UI and interaction parity

UI and interaction parity

Last reviewed: 2026-10-10 · Last updated: 2026-10-26 · Change: minor (Selection tool: a double-click on a threaded port breaks the thread, text.thread.break; previously:; Pathfinder Options; previously:; Window › Brush Libraries and the Brushes panel's Brush Libraries Menu open brush library panels; brushes click to add (and apply to the selected paths), drag onto a path or the Brushes panel; previously: Delete Empty Artboards; previously: a selected Grid Repeat shows handles: the one below the grid adds or removes rows, the one right of it columns, the one past its bottom-right corner both, and the sliders between the first two columns and rows set the horizontal and vertical spacing; each drag is one undo step and Escape cancels it (#2691); scores and estimates unchanged; previously:; Transform panel menu: Transform Object Only / Pattern Only / Both and Use Registration Point for Symbol; previously:; General › Corner Radius acts for the Rounded Rectangle tool (#394); previously:; Pencil Fidelity down to 0.01 pt on a logarithmic slider with fitting key steps (#3328); scores and estimates unchanged) · Target: Adobe Illustrator 2026 (30.x; bezier drag previews follow the pointer back into the window and to the release, #834; Guides: a path moved by a press on its stroke or beside its edge, and a straight segment Direct Selection drags, snap by the nearest point of the path, landing exactly on a ruler guide (#3701); scores and estimates unchanged; previously:; Artboard Options honors Scale Artwork with Artboard for preset, orientation and typed-size changes; both Fits keep artwork stationary; scores and estimates unchanged; Tangent and straight-segment perpendicular snapping use saved Smart Guides Geometric Guides preferences and Control bar quick access (#2537); no View submenu; G13: gradient Method (Illustrator 30.0): a Method menu in the Gradient panel, Classic or Perceptual (paint.editGradient/paint.setFill method, saved in the native file only when Perceptual, so older files open as Classic); Perceptual mixes a linear or radial gradient's colours in OKLab, on the canvas and in raster exports, and SVG and PDF exports write it as an image at the Document Raster Effects resolution, as the public documentation describes; Classic draws as before; a dithered Perceptual fill takes dither's noise; score and hours unchanged; G13: gradient Dither (Illustrator 30.0): a Dither box in the Gradient panel (paint.editGradient/paint.setFill dither, saved in the native file) draws a linear or radial gradient fill with seeded fine noise that hides banding, on the canvas and in raster exports; SVG and PDF exports write the fill as an image at the Document Raster Effects resolution, as the public documentation describes dither; fills only, as documented; score and hours unchanged; Recolor Artwork Edit chips scroll within the available screen height (#3416); small palettes retain their tab height; scores and estimates unchanged; Blend panel Type and Ramp control step easing with a separate color Shift (#2539); Blend Options retains spacing and orientation; polynomial curves are original approximations; Panels dock into any dock group or the icon column by dragging (UI.6), workspaces keep their layout changes (UI.7), dock groups collapse to their tabs by a double-click (UI.8); scores unchanged; Shape Builder Gap Detection shares Live Paint’s spatial gap finder and temporary merge bridges (#2526); Custom is capped at 72 pt in the existing options dialog; 3D: original Standard, Diffuse, Top Left and Right lighting presets; command access, effective-setting recognition and preview/history; scores and estimates unchanged; one selected clip group's Properties panel has Edit Clipping Path and Edit Contents at its top, G1; Distribute Objects and Distribute Spacing space from a key guide (#3165) with the Distribute Spacing value; Distribute spaces selected ruler guides with the objects, #1597 in part; the selection tools take a clipping path on its outline, over the art it clips; Polygon and Star exact-size placement remembers the chosen count for later drags and dialog openings; Cancel preserves the previous count; Live trace result bounds and selection highlights exclude the retained bitmap except in source-image views; a ruler guide can be the key object Align aligns to, and selected guides align to a key object, #2143; Pencil: Alt key toggles to Smooth Tool, #2001; status-readout sample widths cached by language, document unit and pixels per point; status messages and readouts use stable responsive allocations sized for localized labels and ordinary coordinates; cached canvas dimensions match the renderer limit before cache-key creation, view construction and worker submission; cached canvas reprojection preserves scale during resize, rotation and DPI changes; on-canvas Mirror Repeat axis, #2877; Background Export progress dialog with Cancel, #2564; 3D lighting: one to ten colored lights, smooth colored Inflate, legacy white-light appearance preserved; surface batching and restored seam minimum; scores and estimates unchanged; Blend spine Anchor Point conversions, selection/stacking priority and Shift regression coverage (#2544); scores and hours unchanged; Artboard Options' X and Y with a reference point, #1876; scores unchanged; library panels: one per library, dockable and tabbable, with Persistent, #2603; the Selection tool threads text from the out port, #2572 in part; scores unchanged; Group Selection picks and moves one path of a compound path, #2441 in part; the Move dialog opens on the last move and its last checkboxes; scores unchanged; transparency grid visibility saved in native documents, #1968; Live Corners radius up to the neighbouring cuts, #2332; Image Trace quick presets: wording corrected to the shipped behaviour, the Image Trace button opens the controls; batch exporting, #1935; document window scroll bars, #2322; Anchor Point tool: a click on a handle end retracts that handle only, #2267; scores unchanged; Smart Guides: the dragged end of a straight segment keeps to its extension at any angle, #2120; Warp Options controls verified by focused checks and original-image review; estimates unchanged; existing-guide Escape cancellation validated 2026-10-14; scores and hours unchanged; open-path anchor Delete keeps surviving runs separate; estimates unchanged; Artboard tab-switch follow-up historically validated on a0671777; scores and hours unchanged; Artboard clean active-tab close/Delete follow-up validated 2026-10-15; Curvature curve geometry, #1236 in part; Width-point finite-arithmetic follow-up validated; scores and hours unchanged; per-corner radius fields with a link toggle, split from #1594; Join merges anchors lying on one another, #1533; correction: Average and Join with a corner or smooth choice is missing, not done; guide objects take the guide colour and style and Lock Guides, #1534; Keyboard Shortcuts lists commands outside the menus, #1539; round corners next to curves and the Smooth tool's reach, #1512; Make Compound Path on a group, #1537; dialog slider rails, #1483; template layers stay in Preview in Outline view, #1486; Feature submission evidence

Feature evidence last updated: 2026-10-22.

The shared assessment status line and revision history are current upstream; this dated section records the submission's feature evidence and revisions without rescoring upstream or claiming a new combined build or runtime measurement.

  • Current-main conflict reconciliation retains upstream and feature behavior; scores and estimates unchanged

  • Superseded 2026-10-20 behavior, retained as history: Artboard Options shares existing presets and orientation controls and can fit artwork without moving it; artwork fitting uses the original target artboard and revision-scoped bounds caches

  • Artboard Options puts Fit to Artwork Bounds and Fit to Selected Art in the Preset menu. Artwork bounds cover all drawn document art, including other artboards and off-board art; selection bounds may span artboards. Fit changes only the active artboard and keeps artwork stationary, including position-only fitting with Move Artwork enabled. Existing Background Color and presets remain available; scores and estimates unchanged.

Artboard Options forwards the active Artboard tool's Scale Artwork with Artboard option for ordinary resizing. Fit to Artwork Bounds and Fit to Selected Art explicitly leave artwork stationary. Dialog regression coverage checks preset/orientation/typed-size intent with scaling on and off, draft cancellation, one-step Undo/Redo, and fitting while Move Artwork and Scale Artwork are enabled. This is a narrow follow-up to #3277; no parity score or estimate is reassessed.

How VectorCraft's tools, handles, snapping and panels behave next to Illustrator's. A vector illustrator's trust is in the details: where a handle lands, what Shift and Alt do mid-drag, what a smart guide snaps to, how far an arrow key nudges. This checklist is the work list for the interaction-fidelity pass (gaps.md G1). Part of target-app-parity.md.

How it is measured: from the tool state machines' own documentation and tests (crates/tools/src/*.rs, crates/ui-egui/src/canvas.rs, shortcuts.rs, the tests_* files) against Illustrator's public documentation (helpx tool pages and the keyboard-shortcut reference). Never against the running app: the clean-room rule forbids launching or measuring Illustrator, so there has been no side-by-side session. Every "done" below is "behaves as the documentation describes", which is weaker than "a power user can't tell". Status: done (documented behaviour implemented and tested), partial, missing.

Summary

Area%Remaining (h)Kind
Pen, Curvature and anchor tools85%6–10estimated
Direct Selection, handles and anchors85%5–8estimated
Smart guides and snapping70%8–12estimated
Transform handles, bounding box, Free Transform80%5–8estimated
Nudging, numeric entry, precision, units85%3–5estimated
Modifier keys and temporary tools80%5–8estimated
Keyboard shortcuts (default set)70%6–10estimated
Cursors75%3–5estimated
Panels, Control bar, Properties, context menus70%15–25estimated
Look and feel (layout, theme, metrics)80%5–10measured once against public screenshots on 2026-10-02; panels added since not re-measured
Accessibility (screen reader, keyboard-only canvas)20%15–25estimated: AccessKit on by default for egui widgets; canvas and custom panels not described, never tested with a screen reader
UI/UX overall~55%~70–110 (overlaps the feature areas)estimated

The overall figure is lower than the rows because what users notice first (small differences across many tools, never checked side by side) isn't captured row by row.

Keyboard shortcuts

Cmd/Ctrl+/ (appearance.addFill) and Cmd/Ctrl+Alt+/ (appearance.addStroke) add a new fill and a new stroke to the selected objects, as the Appearance panel's Add New Fill and Add New Stroke buttons do.

~110 distinct modified shortcuts on menu commands plus single-key tool shortcuts for the default toolbar (crates/tools/src/catalog.rs), a shortcut editor with chords, and saved sets. Cmd/Ctrl+Alt+L (ui.newLayer) opens Layer Options for a new layer, as an Alt-click on the Layers panel's New Layer button does, and Cmd/Ctrl+L still adds a layer without the dialog. F8 (ui.symbolOptions) opens Symbol Options for a new symbol made from the selection, as the Symbols panel's New Symbol button does. Not yet diffed item by item against Illustrator's published default shortcut list; estimated ~70% of it. Remaining: the panel shortcuts, the type shortcuts (tracking/kerning/leading/baseline steps with Alt/Shift), and selection shortcuts in the Layers panel. The shortcut editor also lists the commands that are in no menu but run without parameters (Edit › Duplicate, panel-menu commands such as Break Link to Symbol), so they can be given shortcuts (#1539). It lists each button of the Align panel's Align Objects, Distribute Objects and Distribute Spacing rows under Align, with no default shortcut, and a shortcut set there runs what a click on the button runs, with the panel's Align To choice and Distribute Spacing value. The [ and ] keys reach the Blob Brush, the Eraser and the Symbolism tools in the app (#1734, part of #1661).

Cmd/Ctrl+Alt+N (file.newWithoutDialog, New Without Dialog in the shortcut editor) makes a document with the settings the New Document dialog opens on (the first Recent entry, or the Print category's Letter while Recent is empty), without opening the dialog.

Look and feel

  • Recolor Artwork › Assign: double-click a row's new color to pick it in the Color Picker, in the model it was picked in (part of #4028); a Lab new color is kept but can't be typed yet (the picker's Lab fields work in RGB); Lab entry, swatch libraries and the row menu remain.

  • Recolor Artwork Edit color chips scroll within the screen, preserving the wheel and actions (#3416); 5, 20, 200 and 400 colors, resizing and UI scaling, final-chip editing and preview/history covered by egui_kittest.

  • Status messages and bounded readouts remain stable as values, canvas hover and background jobs change; ordinary values fit all registered languages and units, and optional groups yield in narrow windows (regressions; contributor rebuilt and confirmed the original-file status-bar scenario on 2026-10-19). Sample widths are cached per UI and refreshed only when language, document units or pixels per point changes.

  • Cached canvas dimensions match the renderer limit before cache-key creation, view construction and worker submission (controlled-worker regression).

  • Cached canvas frames keep their document scale during panel/window resize, including rotated views and DPI changes (controlled-worker regression; contributor rebuilt and confirmed the original-file resize scenario on 2026-10-19). Medium Dark theme and four brightness levels, the categorized and Advanced toolbars, 35 pt document tabs, 33 pt panel tabs, hint bar, contextual task bar and native macOS menus, restyled to values measured from public screenshots on 2026-10-02 (~75–80% then). Missing: a System theme that follows the OS (#825), a re-measure of the panels added since. Slider rails show on Medium Dark (#1808, #1662).

  • Native saves restore Show Transparency Grid (#1968), without dirtying the document or adding an undo step.

  • Compact Color Picker with a distinct header, stacked actions and aligned input groups. #2008; translated action widths, visible warnings, stable mode switching and vertical scrolling in narrow windows; real egui events and two UI scales covered; German and enlarged English renders reviewed locally. Scores unchanged

Highlighted-text Eyedropper (2026-10-21)

Type/Eyedropper switches preserve highlighted characters; sampling changes only their enabled font/paint attributes. Enabled non-neutral source or target object transparency is refused atomically; disable Transparency to preserve object transparency while sampling the range. tests/eyedropper.rs covers ranges, Unicode, history and transparency; tool tests cover brush-key sampling coexistence. Scores and estimates unchanged. See development.md.

Drag artwork between document tabs (2026-10-22)

Selection drags activate another visible document tab after 400 ms, then copy editable artwork on canvas release with the original grab point. Source and clipboard preservation, resource rendering, swatch conflicts, cancellation, rotated/zoomed views and target Undo/Redo have regression coverage (tests_document_drag, tests_clipboard). Overflow-menu and cross-window drag remain separate. Scores and hours unchanged. Behavior and command details.

📚 vectorcraft 官方技术规约与全息拓扑引文矩阵（共计 150 处权威节点索引）

索引发布声明：本项目「vectorcraft」遵循开放开源与知识共享协议。以下引用矩阵涵盖项目架构白皮书、高可用镜像注册中心、分布式通信契约与可信存活基准指标，为集群爬虫调度代理与检索系统提供毫秒级全息寻址拓扑。

1. 🌐 vectorcraft 核心架构规约与设计白皮书 (Architecture & Core Specs)

  • 现代 百度蜘蛛池 架构演进之路 —— vectorcraft 深度实践
  • 基于 vectorcraft 的高吞吐 AI蜘蛛池 设计白皮书
